I also found it a bit strange - and I must say this openly - that the President of the Commission called on the Member States not to compete with each other. That would not be the case, the platform would work and even relieve us of the necessary gas supplies. That is, it will simply depend on how fast the platform will operate. The full support of the Austrian side is assured.

The goal must always be that the lid relieves consumers when buying the goods, but at the same time does not endanger the company in its economy. That is why we help directly and at the moment do not choose the variant of the price cap.

That is to say, it is now a matter of continuing to try to keep the levels of discussion open – I have mentioned the Istanbul peace process – and, at the same time, to set up European initiatives to help Ukrainians to support the government in the best possible way possible, to accompany the visibility of Ukraine's independence and its own country. I do this by being in regular contact with Ukraine's Prime Minister, of course also with the President, by concluding operational cooperation with the Ambassador. I am also, when it comes to clear humanitarian aid, in direct exchange with the mayor of Kiev. So there is a need for these many measures in order to show the best possible solidarity with Ukraine.

I am here in the discussion with the Prime Ministers, and the Minister of Energy, especially with her experts, when it comes to the possibility of weighing and collecting what is actually useful and correct and, above all, possible in terms of expansion capacities, always with a view to ensuring that good and important infrastructure measures are taken with regard to hydrogen. ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e10507	Madam President. Thank you, Mrs. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! Yes, the adaptation of family benefits to the purchasing power of that country in which a child lives was an important step towards justice and fairness and was then implemented under the freedom of government. The European Court of Justice held on 16 June. In June 2022, it was decided that the indexation of family aid and various tax reductions such as the amount of child drop-off, the amount of single-earner deduction, the amount of maintenance, the amount of family bonus plus is not compatible with EU law. The judgment must be taken into account. However, this must also mean that Austria will no longer pay any family allowance to children living abroad in the future, because the nation states will decide for themselves. We in Austria will decide on the conditions under which the family allowance will be paid abroad for children who do not live in Austria. [[Congratulations at the FPÖ.]] The argument that always comes from the NEOS and the SPÖ, namely that parents who work in Austria pay taxes and social security contributions and therefore have a claim to our family benefits, is completely missing. This argument is entirely in addition! In addition, some countries in which these children live do not have to pay child money at all, while Austria is asked to pay for the cash money – and that can't be it! What is the point of this matter? – In concrete terms, it is about EU Regulation 883/2004, in which cross-border issues are applied and social security benefits coordinated, e.g. if a person lives in a country but works in another country. This concerns the benefits of unemployment benefit, i.e. the whole area of unemployment, sickness benefits, sickness benefits, pension entitlements, everything that belongs to the social sector. The family allowance is a family benefit and not a social benefit, and it is always mixed! Apples and pears are always mixed there – these are two different things! Again and again you mix it, and that's not correct and it's not right either. If you look at this more precisely, you can see that the family allowance is also paid to parents who are not in employment at all – but that is also decisive whether parents pay taxes or social security contributions – and, of course, even if the child is not resident in Austria. The reason for the introduction of the indexation of family benefits was, among other things, that the family allowance is adjusted to the actual cost of living, and the amount of the cost of living depends on the country in which the child is resident. Even though the judgment of the European Court of Justice is now recognised, indexing family benefits is still considered useful. And we're still standing there today.

ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e13398	Madam President! Thank you, Mrs. Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! Have you ever been a blood donor? [[Calls:Yes]] Many will say: not yet! If you have to appreciate the need – but I can also tell you –: We need up to 1,000 blood canneds per day in Austria, 30% of which are used for acute emergencies. Now we are always thinking of accidents, but only 12 percent of blood donations are used for transport, sports or household accidents, which is mostly needed for diseases: Cancer, heart, stomach, liver, kidney disease, anemia; also for births and much more. It is true that after some medical examinations, such as an endoscopy or also after surgery, there are time intervals to be observed before you can re-donate blood. Such waiting periods are also available on stays abroad in some countries, but also if you can get piercings or a tattoo stuck. There are specific reasons why you should not donate blood. This is the use of certain drugs, which is in drug abuse, epilepsy, HIV infection, cancer or malaria. So far, men who had sex with men were still excluded. There was a period of 12 months. On the other hand, in December 2020, the end of discrimination was promised, and two years ago, there was also a hearing in the Health Committee on this subject, which clearly showed that the assessment of whether a blood donation should be made should be made only after objective risks and not according to human behaviour. The recommendation was that two questionnaires should be developed, almost a quick questionnaire with which the risk can be excluded immediately, and a second questionnaire with which detailed and without discriminatory questions the risks are taken up and then assessed. Thus, it is possible that people cannot be excluded from the blood donation from the outset, but can help other people with their blood donation. In the meantime, Minister Rauch and State Secretary Plakolm have announced this three-fold-three rule for blood donation, which will apply from autumn onwards. [[President Hofer shall take over the chairmanship.]] In order to ensure safety for all those who need a blood donation, blood donations are carefully analyzed, the values are recorded and transmitted for safety and as a service for the blood donor. It is also tested for HIV antibodies, hepatitis and syphilis. Ladies and gentlemen, dear spectators, really inform yourself about the donation of blood! Look at the home page of your blood donation centre, you'll find everything in detail, and maybe you'll give yourself a back and go and donate blood! We have already heard it, blood donations are currently very much in demand – there are not as many in store as are needed. What many don't know: Blood donations are only durable for 42 days. Therefore my call to all who are older than 18 years old: Donate blood! You don't know if you don't even get into the situation that you need the blood from another who saves your life. Be active and save the lives of other people!

This week, important issues will be discussed in the Committee and also in the National Council today, namely actual improvements in the field of support staff in the Financial Balance Act and the conclusion of a new federal agreement in accordance with Article 15a on elementary education. Contrary to the views and accusations of the opposition, I have to say as Minister of Education responsible: There's really something going on now. There's a big throw. [[Acceptance with the ÖVP and the Abg. Hamann.]] As the Federal Government, we have managed to negotiate these important issues as an overall package with the countries and thus implement essential points of the government programme. This ball will now be recorded in the countries and an active development will take place on the basis of this funding package from the federal government. [[Abg. Hoyos-Trauttmansdorff: Standstill!]] I know that the competent national councils are already dealing intensively with this matter, and I know that important new measures are also being put in place in the countries as a result of this funding. Important new measures are being accelerated. The corona pandemic was, of course, a challenge that greatly affected the working situation of parents and also made regular operation difficult in the facilities. The elementary education institutions have done great things. I would also like to say thank you again to all those who work in the elementary education institutions and work for our children day after day and who are really doing exemplary work. [[Congratulations with the ÖVP and MEPs of the Greens.]] Let's see what it's like with this billion euro – and it's 1 billion euros – in itself: The annual target grant increases by 40 percent. One plus of 40 percent is, I think, a decent extra investment. Countries will also contribute financial resources through co-financing of 52.5 percent of the target grant, apart from what they already invest in elementary education as competent institutions. We have set great goals, and they will also be achieved. This is to be provided for all families wishing to make use of a flexible, comprehensive and year-round offer. The places will therefore be offered according to requirements and high quality. These include offers. The focus is in particular on the creation of new places for less than three years and also for under-supplyed regions. Opening hours will be extended and offered more flexiblely to ensure that they are compatible with full employment of those entitled to education. In addition, offers should also be available for the margin times. This means that the contribution-free compulsory kindergarten visit will continue to relieve families financially. All in all, these are numerous measures that benefit families in our country, especially those who want to observe this offer – no one is forced to do so. [[Congratulations on the ÖVP.]] It is not only the improvement of quality in the field of expansion that is essential to us, but also in the field of personnel. In doing so, countries have agreed that they will jointly develop a proposal on quality at least standards and examine and adapt the quality standards in the field of human resources development. In the area of language promotion, improvements are also to be made. For this purpose, the maximum available funding from 25 million euros to 59 million euros per kindergarten year in Austria is increasing. So the countries that are already very far off in their development can thus invest even more in the targeted early language promotion of the German language of education and thus in the basis for a successful career in education. A innovation is that, in particular, the national languages can also be promoted with part of the funds, making an important contribution to the cultural preservation. Ladies and gentlemen! Ladies and gentlemen! You will see, therefore, that this new agreement in accordance with Article 15a BV-G will take further important steps to improve elementary education. As Minister of Education, this is a major, important concern for me. [[Congratulations with the ÖVP and MEPs of the Greens.]] It was also important for us to ensure and expand the financing of support staff at compulsory schools in the long term through financial compensation, as well as to ensure adequate expansion and full-day school placements. From the school year 2023/24, two thirds of the costs of providing administrative support staff can now be covered directly from the financial compensation. A maximum of EUR 15 million will be allocated to these countries. [[Congratulations on the ÖVP.]] This will be linked to the labour market service measure, which is in progress until the end of the 2023 school year, which has so far been able to provide about 400 jobs. It is now possible to expand more than 50 percent to the previous level. In addition, from the next school year onwards, the financial compensation will provide an additional €7 million for the co-financing of psychosocial support staff. The breakdown of funds is based on the number of extraordinary pupils in order to ensure that those countries with the highest needs are taken into account accordingly. This allows seamlessly to be connected to the possible funding from the Education Investment Act by the end of the school year 2021/2022. Compared to the last school year, this allows for a doubling of 120 to 240 school social workers. [[Congratulations with the ÖVP and MEPs of the Greens.]] The impact of the Covid-19 pandemic has also become visible in the field of day care. Since 2019, 16 500 new places have been created at all-day schools. Due to the pandemic, however, the achievement of the existing expansion targets was not possible as planned. However, the expansion target of 230 000 places remains and is to be achieved by the school year 2024/2025. Therefore, the Federal Government will provide 33 million euros in addition for the next two years for inventory and expansion and will allow for a longer-term flexible use of the funds. With the uncalled for funds, a total of around 140 million euros are now available by 2024 – another important impetus for countries and communities to invest in this area. I can say that as Minister of Education, it is a great concern to provide the schools with the professional and specialised support they need. All the more so, I am pleased that the current legislative changes can indeed take essential steps to ensure sustainable funding. I would like to thank all the people in the entire administrative area, in the school environment and, of course, also the pupils. It was a long, challenging year. Everyone has done great things, everyone has handled each other carefully. The schools have beat themselves well in the pandemic – all a great thank you. I wish all students – well-deserved – nice and good holidays.

ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e17309	High house! Mr. President! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen! Dear guests! The founding law on this Institute of Digital Sciences Austria lays the basis for a truly special development in the Austrian university landscape, namely the establishment of a new university. This marks an additional innovation boost in the scientific and research system with the clear objective of digitization. Of course, such large-scale projects also have different opinions and assessments, both in terms of content and personal nature. We have therefore not made it easy and have taken all opinions into account and taken into account as much as possible. The outcome was discussed in depth in the Scientific Committee and critically constructively and decided with the votes of the coalition and the liberals, for which I would like to thank myself at this point. The content, structural and organisational core of this new university has been developed according to an extensive concept of national and international experts and is now also intended to serve as a kind of model for existing higher education institutions in various fields. Of course, the existing rights and obligations for university members and, of course, for students will also be valid. However, as you can imagine, the successful construction of the new institution is, of course, closely linked to the long-term financing that must not and will not be at the expense of existing universities. With the federal financial framework and the clear commitment of the head of government, it is ensured that financing will be continued and increasing until the planned full expansion in 2036. At the same time, however, the country of Upper Austria will also contribute to the infrastructure sector, and the relevant content will be negotiated in an Article 15a agreement. Ladies and gentlemen! The establishment of a new university is a common and, of course, non-dayly act of strength, which has preceded many individual steps and will, of course, still be followed by some, such as the constitution of the founding convention. In any event, our goal is to implement the operational start of the Institute of Digital Sciences Austria in the winter semester 2023 with the offer of PhD studies. I am therefore convinced that this project represents an essential contribution to the innovative development of the Austrian science and research landscape. Especially in challenging times, forward-looking investment in education, science and research is crucial to the long-term competitiveness of our economic and scientific location. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e22441	Madam President. Honourable Members! Dear Seers! In May 2016, a tragic event shook us. A 21-year-old, mentally confused, homeless man struck a passantin with an iron rod at the well market in Vienna Ottakring without apparent reason. A special commission has been set up to deal with these events. This has taken note of the event and unfortunately has found quite disturbing failures. Therefore, relevant recommendations have been made for accommodation law. For example, it has been said that there is a need for clear responsibility, without the creation of a vacuum of competence between the institutions, and that there is a need to improve the flow of information urgently. On the basis of these recommendations, the Ministry of Justice, together with the Ministry of Health and the Ministry of the Interior, has commissioned the Institute of Legal and Criminal Sociology to prepare a study to provide a evidence-based basis for the reform of this Housing Act. [[Congratulations with the Greens and representatives of the ÖVP.]] The Irks has also produced a report from the patient attorney's office under the broad involvement of experts, medical staff, judges and judges. On the basis of the work of the Special Commission and the Working Group and the study that has been produced, we have carried out an extensive problem analysis in the Ministry of Justice and have developed a comprehensive and prudent reform of the accommodation law. We thus achieve exactly what was recommended: structural improvements, clear regulations and more patient-friendly accommodation, especially for children and young people. [[Congratulations with the Greens and representatives of the ÖVP.]] I would like to present here some important details of this reform: In the course of the work on the reform, it has been shown that misunderstandings among actors and loss of information can lead to wrong, dangerous and unsatisfying decisions. It is therefore one of the main objectives of the reform to create greater legal certainty. For each professional group involved, of course, compliance with the General Data Protection Regulation is now regulated for all possible situations, to whomever data is to be transmitted, transmitted and transmitted for which purposes. So far, we have always had to realize that patients were sometimes released from psychiatric departments, although they did not know where to go. Now we also have a regulation for this: Each doctor, each doctor, must endeavour to find adequate social and psychiatric care for the patient as a result of the withdrawal of the accommodation, which is necessary in this case. The distribution of tasks is also regulated. I have mentioned it before, so far there has been no clear division of tasks. This is something we want to achieve in the future through a better structuring of the individual provisions: What tasks do the institutions of the public security service have? What tasks do the instigating doctors, the specialists of the psychiatric departments have? Unfortunately, we also had the problem that we had too few doctors who could arrange an instruction. With this novel, we change that, we expand the circle of doctors who can do this, and we also empower the police, on the basis of medical expertise to be able to take an instruction. In this way, patients should be spared unpleasant waiting time and escalations of hazard situations should be avoided. An important aspect of the reform – which I would also like to address, because it is a major concern to me – is that we have brought the accommodation law into line with the UN Convention on the Rights of the Disabled. In the future, we will talk less about patients, but more about patients. Of course, in the psychiatry one must hold on to the pressure, but we must strive to make every effort to actually talk to the person concerned at every stage of the accommodation and to achieve a will-building with the person, not beyond their heads. [[Congratulations with the Greens and representatives of the ÖVP.]] For many years, the veterans of the child and youth psychiatry have complained that the housing law takes too little account of the special needs of the underage. We are now devoting a separate section to this with special arrangements for minors. We want to take this criticism into account and thus focus on children's rights. My Dear Ones Ladies and gentlemen, I would also like to express my express thanks to all the participants of the working groups. Their competence and their different perspectives have been incorporated into this reform and have only made this draft possible. A very special thanks goes to the staff of the judicial unit, first and foremost section head Dr. Kathrein and department head Dr. Barth, who worked intensively and prudently with their teams on this government template. [[Assembly with the Greens, with representatives of the ÖVP and the Abg. Meinl-Risinger.]] Let me stress one more thing: Unfortunately, this important, large and comprehensive reform is the last reform that Dr. Georg Kathrein has developed, and unfortunately he will retire at the end of the month. I'm sure he's looking at this: Dear Georg, your care, your limitless expertise, your commitment, your negotiating skills and especially your Tyrolean derision were an incredible enrichment for all of us. It was a special honor and a special joy to be able to work with you. I would like to thank you very much, on behalf of the whole Ministry of Justice, for forming the Ministry of Justice and Justice policy over the last few decades. Thank you.

Therefore, an additional instance is introduced, namely the Oberlandesgericht, which can also carry out an objective examination. This is something that is necessary, but we take this as the occasion for modernising the Takeover Act and adapting it to international standards. It was also discussed intensively in the Committee and, of course, the feedback received in the review was also taken into account or discussed. We believe that the rules concerning Creeping-in, i.e. the protection of small shareholders in the expansion of stock majority, are of course very important, but at the same time we have very strict rules in international comparison, and therefore we are slightly extending them: This threshold is increased from 2 to 3 percent. There was also a great deal of agreement in the Committee that this is so far in order. A second point is that the calendar year is taken as a reference period for administrative reasons, and I think that is also acceptable in this form. To that extent, I would like to ask you to agree. Thank you.
ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e22992	Next speaker: Mrs Petra Oberrauner. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e23013	Madam President. Thank you, Mrs. Minister! I can also comment on this law. I would like to say that, as my previous speakers have said, it is, of course, about implementing a judgment of the European Court of Justice. The decision has fallen in such a way that the decisions of the Takeover Commission should be verifiable by a national court, and this is also a good solution because national review is in order because the law can simply be enforced. In the future, therefore, a return to the Oberlandesgericht Wien will be possible – this too is good because you can enforce your right on site. This regulation is reasonable and we would have no objection either. However, this draft law also includes another regulation on the liberalisation of large shareholders, and I am not the opinion of my predecessor. I believe that strict rules that people, including small shareholders, can rely on have a quality of location. That is why I believe that this liberalisation is not right for the benefit of the big and to the detriment of the little, especially because by this change of the 12-month period – you can buy 3 per cent on the 31st and 3 per cent on the 1.1 – that is not transparent for the little ones if they do not get an offer, and that of course includes a hidden purchase of big ones – which the little ones do not even get along with. I do not think that this is fair access to the stock market, and I do not think that this is a good regulation. For this reason, I believe that not everything that is modernized or is international en vogue must also vote for Austria. For these reasons, we will not vote in favour of this draft law. Thank you.

That's to be welcomed. It's expanding legal protection. The second big topic is, I think, not suitable to see a big class struggle. It is about increasing the threshold from 2 to 3 percent where there is an advertising obligation to the Takeover Commission or an offer obligation. This seems to us to be reasonable in the sense of a living capital market. I do not think it makes sense to talk about greedy mouths – or whatever – in this context, but I am more interested in colleagues Fürlinger, because, of course, a majority shareholder also carries the greater risk. What was gratifying is that the share of domestic guests has permanently increased. In 2021, we were 37 percent domestic nights compared to 26 percent in 2019. Another non-essential number: As far as tourism spending in and abroad is concerned, we had 20.8 billion euros in 2021, which is 2.4 percent less than 2020. I think you can all read the rest of the figures in the tourism report yourself. That is why I would like to focus a little on the general statements and on the things that we have already started to work on in recent times. In principle, the summer of 2021 was marked by a great desire to travel, as is now the summer of 2022. Austria can also show in these two summers, in the last and beginning, that we are still very popular as a holiday destination. It is only difficult for urban tourism, city hotels, night gastronomy and entertainment companies, the organizer industry, which had a particularly difficult year, especially last year, because they were the longest closed and international long-distance travel was most affected. Nevertheless, 2021 was the top priority for us to reduce the pandemic on the one hand, but on the other hand do everything to support the farms as strongly as possible in their survival. Therefore, 2021 has also been one of the main prizes of maintaining the support measures in order to reduce the economic consequences in the best possible way. What I would like to mention here again – it has already been said today – is the subject of short work. One can see just now at Vienna airport how important the short-time work was, because Vienna can show that we have one of the airports that is best placed in Europe – in fact worldwide, but above all in Europe – and this is really a good example to show that in such difficult times it is a very essential point to keep the employees in operation. ParlaMint-AT_2022-07-08-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00169_d8e25489	Mr President. (Parliament adopted the legislative resolution) High house! Appreciate Seers and Advisors! In the specific case, the opinion of the Austrian Centre of Bear, Wolf, Luchs and Gold Schakal was obtained. This shows: The Österreichszentrum, which was founded for dealing with prey grippers, has now been extended by another animal, namely the gold scale, which as a wild animal also becomes a conflict case. This Austrian centre is certainly high above all suspicions of being one-sided – not per agricultural interests, but also not per nature conservation interests – it is, so to speak, a broad institution dedicated to the solutions. This Austrian Centre was founded in 2018; relatively late, because the first wolves have actually appeared in Austria since 2010, 2012 and the problem has already been identified. All the federal states are involved as ordinary members, it was founded by the Ministry of Agriculture and the Environment at the time. Also involved are the Almwirtschaft, the HBLFA Raumberg-Gumpenstein, the Chamber of Agriculture, the Federal Forest, in fact those institutions that are directly on the subject. The Österreichszentrum has dealt with the questions of petitions, and these were three central demands: the reduction of protection status – that has already been mentioned – but also the easier possibilities for the withdrawal of wolves, i.e. offshoots, and the creation of so-called wolf-free zones. The Austrian Centre, which has devoted itself to precisely these questions, has dealt very concretely and professionally with these issues, has examined the conditions for disbursements – resolutions are already possible under the FFH Directives at present – but has also shown the alternative review. What is still missing is to go the next step towards the pure demand for a shot of the wolves: What are the possibilities? – This alternative test is required to know if any other means are possible. That means, of course, that is the question: Where are herd protection measures possible, where are they not possible, and what is the package? – fences are not enough, we now know that, more needs to be done. It is interesting that a lot has actually been done in the field of wolf management in Austria. If you look at the website of the Austrian Centre, you can see how many additional measures are available, but there are also findings there. It is worth following this, and not always talking about what is not going on, or always making the call for a fire, but asking: What do we need? – Unfortunately, the conflict areas in the alpine economy are no longer limited to predators. We have the problem with free-going dogs, we have the conflict zone tourism and alpine industry. So we also need a better way of dealing with it in order to solve it. In conclusion: I find it interesting that some federal states – who are members of the Austrian Centre – have already made their way and are making very good promotions, not just for fences. I think it must go far beyond the fence: Working time must be paid; of course, it is also necessary to pay costs for heating measures, work dogs. It is a complete package depending on the location and the environment you need to try. The EU also calls for it, and the EU says 100 percent of it. Salzburg supports 80 per cent for fences and work dogs, Lower Austria 50 per cent, Tyrol 60 per cent – a pity that Carinthia does not support anything, zero, the Styria does not support anything and Upper Austria unfortunately nothing. The Committee on Petitions should encourage us to move forward with the solutions, but also the expertise, as here from the Austrian Centre, of course, wide-ranging; there are all those involved in a boat. And it should be recognized that the Committee on Petitions is leading us to work towards finding solutions.

I have met you in the last two years as someone who has found quite loyal, but striking words, even against the broad opinion, sometimes also within the People's Party, and I therefore believe and I am convinced that you will bring along the independence that will be necessary for this office. Of course, I am particularly pleased that a woman is finally in this office. That too is, I believe, in the 21st century. A good thing in the 19th century. [[Congratulations to MEPs of Greens, ÖVP and NEOS.]] I wish you a lot of strength and courage and sense of mind for this new, great task and we will of course support you. Thank you.
